**Computer Use repair TODO — live implementation tracker**

Companion to [the detailed audit](docs/audits/2026-09-04-mac-computer-use/AUDIT.md). Baseline: main at 603e79e9f877a4a1c9a0b462d8db4da06dc46cf2. Status: in progress. This is the authoritative working TODO for the repair goal. A checkbox is completed only after the implementation and its stated verification are recorded here. The audit checklist is retained as the original plan.

**Current execution plan — compile, test, then accept**

Testing was authorized September 12, with a deadline of September 13 end of day. The target is **12 GB total Mac RAM**, lightweight inference and low heat. Use TDD, one build or inference at a time, one build job, and no source edits during builds. Fara Q4 remains selected; Q6 stays diagnostic under 5 GiB, with production at 4 GiB. The calendar timeout now has a measured native failure and passing repair: read-only screenshot preparation gets a separate finite 10-second deadline; ordinary DOM/input checks keep five seconds. The public post-scroll capture completes in 6.12 seconds. Four local deadline/cancellation variants, three existing cancellation/Stop/takeover methods and scripted Unicode/login/Resume pass. Native Left/Right, Shift selection and Backspace now pass a generic Unicode correction workflow, sixteen field/control variants, existing Tab/paging checks and scripted login/Resume. These are shared writable-text operations, with no site-specific logic. Per-pixel capture cost remains unresolved. See [caret evidence](docs/audits/2026-09-04-mac-computer-use/native-browser-caret-results.json) and [snapshot deadline evidence](docs/audits/2026-09-04-mac-computer-use/native-browser-snapshot-deadline-results.json).

Native desktop capture is now measured: one production capture returned the correct Retina display and fixture window in 35 ms; OCR confirms both independent native field labels. Literal desktop key decoding now passes ten chords across Fara, GUI-Owl and Holo. Browser-only Back compatibility remains explicit and passes native history traversal plus protocol/history regressions. The raw native image exceeded the model PNG cap; that failure now has a passing native capture-to-request repair. The 3456×2234 image becomes 2048×1323 and 1,119,928 PNG bytes, with 29 ms capture plus 152 ms image/request preparation in this probe. Independent fixture labels survive encoding; Fara, Holo and GUI-Owl requests accept the image. Three geometry/orientation/byte-budget methods and three existing request regressions pass. A native desktop executor candidate now passes a real AppKit form workflow with five verified input dispatches, exact Unicode replacement, simulated login/Resume and no replay. Approval rejection/reuse and cancellation before/after dispatch have passing focused checks. The candidate is not selected by the production loader. Fara now has a native Mac capability profile, but its first actual-model native trial failed after two proposals and a lost local-worker connection; native model acceptance remains open. See [native image evidence](docs/audits/2026-09-04-mac-computer-use/native-desktop-image-results.json). See [native desktop evidence](docs/audits/2026-09-04-mac-computer-use/native-desktop-admission-results.json).

**General computer use is the acceptance target.** Production fixes must apply to shared operations and capabilities; website domains, selectors, fixture values and task answers belong only in tests. The selected Fara loader currently creates an owned-WebKit executor, so separate AX form editing or MCP desktop successes do not prove trained-model desktop control. Prioritize that integration gap and unsupported generic input actions alongside the remaining browser failures. Distinguish model proposals, adapter execution, final results and interruptions in every trial. Keep the movie failure as a regression sentinel, with a fixed cross-domain set covering native forms, native documents, cross-app transfer, reference research, travel forms and product catalogues. Full model workflows, provider login, physical phone, whole-app resources and production acceptance remain open.

The latest shared repairs have executed red/green evidence: unrelated native object creation no longer discards unchanged field references; destroyed label objects cannot silently become unlabeled authority; window completion checks exclude partially covered pixels outside their bounds. The controlled native Unicode/login/Resume workflow still passes. Actual Holo now types into the correct field and preserves the sibling, but both complete-task trials remain failed. The latest cancellation also returns before its diagnostic probe has finished joining; all 13 retained probe reports eventually confirm worker exit and credential removal. Fix that diagnostic ownership gap before more actual-model trials. See [AX lifecycle and window evidence](docs/audits/2026-09-04-mac-computer-use/native-ax-creation-and-window-pixels-results.json).

This is the single current verification tracker. **Implemented** means the scoped source change exists; **compiled** requires a successful build of the current code; **tested** requires executed relevant checks; **accepted** requires the stated workflow/product criteria. A dash means that stage is unproved. Historical checkboxes below retain the evidence recorded at their checkpoint and do not promote this branch to a later stage.

**Current execution evidence**

- **Native focus observations repaired through TDD:** The first-frame six-second click control passes. New tests then reproduce missing editor information in Fara/GUI requests and every real native callback. All providers now retain `focused_editor` with the corresponding screenshot and outcome. It contains bounded Accessibility role/label/identifier data, not AXValue or selected text; null represents an unidentified writable editor. Native descriptions explain that a rejected click does not move focus. Browser requests reject mixed native metadata. Two green methods and six bounds/cancellation/history regressions pass. The initial Holo test history used the wrong wait argument name and was corrected before green; the first helper build also had an actor-isolation error. Both failed attempts remain in the evidence.
- **Model behavior remains unaccepted:** GUI-Owl's repeated trial still produces `TokyoTokyoTokyo` in Origin and an empty Destination, despite every input correctly reporting Origin focus. Holo's explicit 5 GiB diagnostic clicks Destination successfully once, then rejects typing and two further clicks; both fields remain empty and the form stays open. Fara Q4 under the same diagnostic allowance identifies failed focus and tries Tab after two misses; all actions reject and both fields stay empty. Holo peaks at 4,505,387,008 bytes and Fara at 4,323,737,600. All workers exit and remove credentials. Production remains Fara with its 4 GiB cap; these trials do not qualify a 12 GB Mac.
- **Next failure is narrowed, not fixed:** Holo and Fara report changed focused authority after inference despite unchanged visible editor labels/frames. A fifteen-second no-inference control at Holo's native click position passes exact typing, Unicode replacement, simulated login/Resume and no replay. Its independent AX target ID/value/selection remain stable; two destruction notifications affect other objects. Waiting alone does not reproduce the rejection. Use the existing nativeTextTarget injection seam to capture AX return codes and lifecycle events across one model call, with no generated effects. Determine whether a binding was retired, an AX read failed, or focus/selection changed before altering admission. Do not add a blanket post-click keyboard block: it would suppress the keyboard recovery Fara attempted. [Focus and diagnostic evidence](docs/audits/2026-09-04-mac-computer-use/native-focus-observation-results.json) retains all ten attempts and three model runs. Three newly passing logical methods raise the conservative historical minimum to **318 Mac / 10 iOS**; the strengthened/renamed first-click control is not counted twice.

- **Shared native provider contracts now pass:** GUI-Owl success status, failure status and manual user control remain distinct through the native executor. The original handoff message survives, Resume produces a fresh observation, and terminal replay sends no further proposals or input. Native Holo and GUI-Owl profiles now declare only implemented Mac actions; each retains its own wire/history protocol. Two executed red failures, three green methods, five regressions and a harness rebuild pass. No production website names, fixture labels/values, task answers or coordinate corrections were added.
- **Native comparison still has no accepted model:** The unchanged form task and 4 GiB cap produce separate failures. GUI-Owl returns six proposals and executes three typing actions in Origin after rejected clicks: final Origin is `TokyoTokyoTokyo`, Destination is empty. It also proposes closing the form against the task constraint; that close is rejected. Peak worker memory is 3,392,421,888 bytes, and all six workers exit and remove credentials. Holo exceeds the same cap before returning an action: its supervisor records 4,312,137,728 bytes; both fields stay empty and its worker exits. Fara remains eligible. Alternative probes start a fresh worker per proposal; Fara retains its worker, so residency/performance results are not equivalent.
- **Inference-free click isolation:** Delayed clicks near the left side of a native editable row pass both with a populated sibling and an empty sibling, followed by exact Unicode replacement and simulated login/Resume. The empty variant includes an initial no-effect observation, so this does not reproduce the actual model's first-frame rejection. No native admission change is justified yet. Retain exact first-click target/pixel evidence next, then address general recovery from failed focus and instruction constraints. Four new passing methods bring the conservative historical minimum to **315 Mac / 10 iOS**. [Native provider and model evidence](docs/audits/2026-09-04-mac-computer-use/native-candidate-contracts-results.json) retains all nine attempts, including one compiler-only failure and both failed model trials. Production routing, complete tasks, provider login, physical phone and whole-app 12 GB/thermal acceptance remain open.

- **Generic text correction repaired through TDD:** The first ArrowLeft proposal failed with unsupportedDocument. Native left/right arrows, Shift selection and Backspace now use the existing one-shot key-event path. Admission requires a retained writable input/textarea with native selection; unrelated page changes may use existing editing checks, while focus, exact selection/value, identity, geometry, authentication and owner checks remain. There are no domain, selector or goal-answer branches in production.

- **Holo protocol and history repairs use TDD:** Holo reads one native OpenAI tool call and accepts completion only from `answer.arguments.content`. The request keeps three images and binds tool results and exact user replies. Native history tests expose two defects: Fara-XML question detection discarded Holo questions; middle-history eviction attached a removed URL proposal's rejection to an earlier wait. Recorded typed action metadata and original following-observation fields fix both, while Fara question/rejection regressions pass. Isolated regeneration now retains the required sources. The Unicode fixture initially assumed one DOM input event, referred to the browser's `origin` property, and decoded form-encoded `+` as literal plus. The retained final screenshot clearly says “MINER AUDITORIUM”; the exact venue assertion remains failing. This is a completed execution path with a model-reading error, not a passed task or a reason to exclude Fara without comparison. The [pinned upstream page-reading action](https://github.com/microsoft/fara/blob/a675d6d61c41c47ae87bacefeab22caad18e3e84/src/fara/agents/fara/fara15_agent.py#L748) extracts page markdown and returns a bounded text observation; the app still parses but does not execute that action. The frozen trajectory regression now reproduces this failure; native text-reading execution is the next bounded repair. Default-composition/provider login, remaining native actions/questions, travel/movie, whole-app/phone lifecycle and actual 12 GB/heat/release qualification remain open.

- **Fara task/Resume checkpoint:** the previous goal turn was progress: it verified native Fara click/type/login admission and stale-observation retirement. This turn connected a Fara-specific task executor to the existing host/browser/client control path, preserving the current user goal separately from assistant text, retaining bounded native image/response history, checking the current scene before input and final model assessment, and saving private fsync-backed journals before native dispatch. They cover no-overlap text→Fara replacement, cached ownership, old-contract rejection, response alias/role/finish validation, retained goals/history, malformed inputs, cancellation cleanup, late old-endpoint cancellation, wrong projector and the 12 GiB launch floor. `fara-managed-probe-build-01` compiled the current app/client core/tests. `fara-managed-proposal-01` then rehashed the pinned model/projector, ran the actual model through the production runtime and transport, decoded its `[392, 540]` click, checked independent DOM bounds and joined worker shutdown. It passed in 13.22 s including test setup/hash verification; runtime startup was 1.60 s and generation 8.18 s. Across 35 samples, max(resident size, physical footprint) peaked at 4,207,738,880 bytes (3.92 GiB), under the retained 4 GiB ceiling. No input was dispatched. Whole-app/multi-image/12 GB/thermal acceptance and Fara browser execution remain open. Evidence: `.build/repair-verification/fara-managed-runtime-verification.json`.

- **Two production capture defects were reproduced and repaired:** native ScreenCaptureKit encodes an absent presenter overlay as `CGRect.null` (infinite origin, zero size); the earlier finite-only parser rejected every such frame. Only that exact sentinel now means no overlay. A real complete sample can also arrive a fraction of a millisecond before its display timestamp. [Apple defines this timestamp as the time WindowServer displays the frame](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/screencapturekit/scstreamframeinfo/displaytime). The native path now waits on its capture queue for at most one 60 Hz interval, then applies the unchanged no-future metadata validator. Excessive leads, interrupted/early waits, malformed rectangles and real overlays remain rejected. `capture-null-overlay-01` and `capture-presentation-admission-01` passed. `native-capture-08` then passed with a real 1188×768 frame: its callback preceded the display timestamp, admission followed it, and the 594×384-point content rectangle covered the complete 2× output surface. No pixels were saved or encoded. The separate verification host was granted capture through the authenticated System Settings picker; the installed production host was untouched. Earlier failed results and the two corrected diagnostic compile errors are retained.
- **Previous foreground blocker (resolved by model-browser-N02-02):** `model-browser-N02-01` ran the production coordinator against one selected frozen local form. The page loaded once, but UserNotificationCenter retained focus, so the browser refused input; both original values remained unchanged and no edit event occurred. The detailed browser report records this cause; XCTest's final error was an additional idle-to-failed teardown transition. The runtime was shut down. The user has been asked to complete or dismiss the system notification because automatic approval review previously rejected controlling that window. The user subsequently dismissed the notification; the same selected case and the model-driven visible login workflow now pass, as recorded above.

The user explicitly requires lightweight inference targeting Macs with **12 GB RAM**, superseding the former 8 GB target. Treat this as the supported product target to qualify, not merely a minimum-RAM install check. Account for the host, browser/WebContent, helper, capture/OCR, model weights and working memory together, with room for macOS and normal user work. The current 32 GB development machine must not hide excessive model residency or compute cost. Ordinary supported tasks should avoid sustained maximum fan load; this needs resource measurements as well as correct results. No 12 GB or thermal qualification has been established.

Exit criterion: two fields can receive the same value, two searches can submit, and pausing after any step does not replay completed work. An uncertain effect is neither auto-retried nor reported as complete.

Exit criterion: all critical regressions pass, all supported task families meet their agreed success targets, and the release report clearly distinguishes successful completion, appropriate intervention, unsupported scope, and execution failure.

**Concrete acceptance cases**

| ID | Scenario | Required observation |
|---|---|---|
| T01 | Type exact leading/trailing spaces and newlines | Exact intended value is preserved |
| T02 | Type emoji, combining marks, and non-Latin text | No corrupted or lost graphemes; verified result |
| T03 | Insert versus replace a selection | Correct mode and field contents |
| T04 | Fill two fields with the same city | Two distinct verified steps; no duplicate suppression |
| T05 | Perform two searches in one task | Two legitimate Return actions, each bound to its step |
| T06 | Drop some synthesized input in a controlled fixture | Partial text detected; no false success |
| T07 | Pause midway through typing, then resume | Current field reconciled without duplicate prefix |
| T08 | Navigate or replace the field at the same position | Stale edit rejected |
| T09 | Type a 2,000-character literal | Exact edit or explicit no-effect preflight rejection |
| T10 | App validation/autocorrection/IME changes the input | Verifier detects meaningful divergence |
| T11 | Target on another display with negative origin | Correct target/display or explicit unsupported result |
| T12 | Move or resize the target during grounding | Old coordinates are never used |
| T13 | Animate an unrelated part of the window | Stable target remains actionable |
| T14 | Change a background window with matching labels | Focused task is unaffected |
| T15 | Two same-labelled controls or browser tabs | Context resolves the target or asks for clarification |
| T16 | Tiny icons and dense tables at multiple scales | Measured target accuracy; appropriate crops |
| T17 | Low-confidence OCR for numbers/currencies | Re-observation or uncertainty, never invented precision |
| T18 | Local/remote takeover during OCR, model load, drag, typing | Bounded stop latency and released held input |
| T19 | Duplicate/delayed approval after pause | No action under the stale approval |
| T20 | Crash before, during, and after an effect | Verified recovery or explicit uncertain outcome |
| T21 | Localized Send/Continue and deceptive search context | Correct effect policy and exact approval boundary |
| T22 | Stale success banner and half-completed compound task | No premature task completion |
| T23 | Slow loading and a repeated no-op | Bounded useful waiting; clear deadline/stuck outcome |
| T24 | Apple unavailable and near-limit prompt context | Honest capability handling; tested budget behavior |
| T25 | Missing visual model but valid structured operation | Independent capability remains available |
| T26 | Network transition, lease expiry, reconnect | Consistent ownership and no duplicate task/effect |
| T27 | Physical low-memory Mac under browser/media load | Measured latency/memory and deliberate degradation |
| T28 | Actual production manifest through mobile-to-Mac flow | No stubbed planner/grounder or wrong-package substitution |
| T29 | Answer a field/value clarification without repeating the original request | Exact original goal retained, fresh target binding, one execution; stale or unrelated answers cannot inherit authority |

Use native/browser fixtures and test accounts for controlled effects. Real user emails, purchases, or credential entry are not required to exercise the transaction and approval design.

**Measurement contract**

For each attempted task, record one terminal outcome: completed and independently verified; user intervention required; unsupported; failed; or uncertain effect. Also record action count, model calls, recovery count, approval count, cold/warm end-to-end and per-stage latency, peak memory pressure, and takeover latency.

Choose product targets before optimization. As a starting proposal, require zero false completions and zero unauthorized effects in the release corpus, all critical deterministic regressions passing, and at least 95% independently verified success on the explicitly supported task families. These are proposed gates, not measured current results or proof of universal safety. Report trial counts and uncertainty; a small hand-picked passing demo is insufficient.

**The first three changes to implement**

1. Repair test discovery/current-package gates and create the nonprivileged core test target.
2. Add TaskContract/Step state with correct repeated-action identity and pause/resume reconciliation.
3. Deliver the verified TextEdit path on one native form and one browser form.

Only then broaden capabilities and evaluate whether an additional model pays for its latency and memory.
